# Mosvold Boutique Hotels — Complete Website Data

_Everything from mosvoldhotels.com — all 75 pages, offers, contacts & images_

## 📞 Contact Directory

- **Reservations:** +94 77 335 8800
- **Emails:** info@mosvold.lk, hr@mosvold.lk
- **Head Office:** 285/2a, Kaduwela Road, Battaramulla, Sri Lanka

**Properties**

- **Mosvold Villa** — Ahangama
  - Address: 38, Welhengoda, Ahangama, Sri Lanka
  - Phone: +94 77 335 8800
  - GPS: 5.9763598, 80.35353
- **Sundara by Mosvold** — Balapitiya
  - Address: 46/21, Brahakmana Waththa, Balapitiya 80550, Sri Lanka
  - Phone: +94 74 112 2110
  - From Colombo: ~100 km, ~2 hours

## 💰 Prices & Booking

Room rates are **not published on the website** — they are served live per check-in/check-out date by the external booking engine **BookingEye**:

- Mosvold Villa: https://mosvoldboutiquehotels.bookingeye.net/rooms?pr=1&ci=<DD-MM-YYYY>&co=<DD-MM-YYYY>
- Sundara: https://mosvoldboutiquehotels.bookingeye.net/rooms?pr=2&ci=<DD-MM-YYYY>&co=<DD-MM-YYYY>

There is therefore no fixed price list on the site; a rate only exists once dates are chosen.

Mosvold Boutique Hotels

## Luxury Beachfront Hotels in Sri Lanka | Mosvold

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/about-us/

> Experience the best luxury hotels and beachside villas in Sri Lanka with Mosvold, where unforgettable views and exceptional service await. Book your stay now!

![Mosvold Villa](https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/05/About-Mosvold-1920x600-1.jpg)
scroll down

Our Story
Luxury with a conscience: When our founders conceptualised hospitality on the Southern Coast of Sri Lanka, we wanted to build a brand that gives back to the community where we thrive. A never-ending give and take, much like the yin and yang of the ocean from which we gain endless inspiration, our ethos for luxury is grounded in genuine care for anyone who knows us.
To us, true luxury is a meaningful sense of community and belonging. It is a dedication to focus on humanity and generosity for all.
Our first boutique hotel was built in the sleepy coastal town of Ahangama in 2012, where we welcomed travellers looking for a culturally immersive luxury experience rooted in nature and community.
Today, we’ve expanded our hospitality to four properties, each with a unique offering that make it an extra special destination. In addition, the entire stretch of Southern Coast from Balapitiya to Ahangama has evolved to centre a multitude of experiences catering to diverse preferences: from local artistic expressions of devil mask making and wood carving to adventure water sports such as surfing and quiet tributes to nature in the forms of visiting tea and cinnamon plantations to conserving nature in turtle hatcheries.
Our Vision
Significantly, you

Our Mission
We strive to understand YOU, the individual, your preferences and choices, closely enough; so that we can do our best to cater to such in our pursuit of creating superior value for the key stakeholders of this business: customers, employees, suppliers and investors, while playing an enabling role in society as a responsible corporate citizen.

Certified Excellence
Discover Our Retreats
Who we are
The Mosvold legacy begins with three individuals whose expertise adds to a single-minded dedication to the highest of standards. With a vision to create lavish luxury while constantly engaging local communities, Mosvold aims to build a culture of experiential luxury centred around our passion for people. This starts with our dedicated team, welcoming you to the center of our world from wherever you travel.
Founder / Chairman of Mosvold Hotels
Nimal Martinus
Founder / Managing Director of Mosvold Hotels
Nilanka Martinus
Founder / Deputy Chairman of Mosvold Hotels
Kurt Mosvold

# Mosvold Villa (Ahangama)

## Luxury Boutique Hotel in Ahangama | Mosvold Villa

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/mosvold-villa/

> Discover the perfect getaway in Ahangama at Mosvold Villa, where our luxury bungalows offer an unforgettable experience. Book now!

Breathtaking ocean views, modern amenities and plush interiors are ideal for a tropical beachside getaway.

![Home Page](https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/mosvold-villa/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2025/09/Mosvold-hotels-Mosvold-Villa-v2-1920x1000-1.jpg)
scroll down

Mosvold Villa
Immerse yourself in the natural beauty and laid-back coastal vibes of Ahangama. Set on a secluded beach, this oceanfront hideaway beckons to those seeking quiet, barefoot, slow & luxury. From the moment you walk in, you know you are somewhere truly soul-soothing.

For Exclusive Rewarding Benefits
Best Rate Guaranteed
No Hidden Costs
Excursion Offers
Where Slow Living Starts
With 16 exquisite rooms overlooking the shimmering waters of the Indian Ocean, Each room has uninterrupted sea views and is only a few steps from the beautiful Ahangama beach.

Unforgettable Experiences Await
Ahangama is a place that rekindles the senses. Connect with culture, community, nature, and the culinary arts in and around our property to delve deeper into your Sri Lankan experience.

Discover Special Deals
Unlock our experiences with these curated packages that add extra value to your stay at Mosvold

Insights & Inspiration
Browse our blog to take a sneak peek into our destinations. We have things to do, sights to see, people to meet, food to taste-we hardly ever run out of stories to tell.

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/mosvold-villa/dining/

> Experience exceptional beachfront dining in Ahangama, with fresh, locally sourced flavours and outstanding Sri Lankan hospitality at Mosvold Villa. Book now!

Sip & Slide Beverage Menu
Pull up a chair where the sun meets the sea at our bar. Try your favourite drinks with a twist of Sri Lankan spice.

scroll down

The Flavours of Our Heritage
Food is our love language. When you think of Sri Lankan hospitality, you immediately think of the warmth, care and nourishment that you get from the food that we lovingly prepare.
“Home-cooked meals” have a special place in our hearts. No matter where we go, food always connects us back to home.
Whether it is nourishment from the land or the sea, coastal areas of Sri Lanka are gastronomical adventures waiting to be Explored.
Another Kind of Depth
Dine with the soothing sound of waves as your backdrop, or enjoy a cozy indoor setting with panoramic ocean views.
Savour the Southern Shore
Relish in the diverse flavours of the Sri Lankan South, fostering an atmosphere for guests to convene and partake in the authentic spirit of the island.

## Unique Stays & Luxury Activities in Sri Lanka | Mosvold Villa

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/mosvold-villa/experiences/

> Experience unique stays and luxury activities, including wellness, spa, and more at Mosvold Villa. Book now!

Sailing in the South
Mosvold Villa

Explore stunning coastal destinations like Mirissa, Galle, and Weligama

scroll down

A Destination to Discover
Ahangama is a place that rekindles the senses. Connect with the culture, community, and nature around our property to delve deeper into what you see.
Sailing in the South
Mosvold Villa

Explore stunning coastal destinations like Mirissa, Galle, and Weligama

Cinnamon Tour
Mosvold Villa

The Cinnamon experience in Koggala lets you explore the ancient spice in an experiential journey. Take the trails of the planter and be treated to a tea and lunch inspired by Cinnamon.

Monastery & Temple Walks
Mosvold Villa

Enter serene temples where monks chant blessings and meditate. Stroll these spiritual spaces and learn the ways of the Buddha and eternal peace.

Whale and Dolphin Watching
Mosvold Villa

Glimpse the largest living creature on the planet. The tour takes you over 20-30Km into the sea to witness the natural habitat of these magnificent creatures along with an abundance of Dolphins.

Madol Duwa Boat Safari
Mosvold Villa

Take a short boat trip to Madol Duwa island, where a famous Sri Lankan story comes to life.

Stilt Fishing
Mosvold Villa

Fishing on a stilt - also known as “Ritipanna” in Sinhala is a very difficult yet rewarding technique to catch fish. Watch fishermen perch on these stilts and even get on one - if you can.

- Duration : 1-3 hours
- Participant Capacity : Any Number
Ahangama beaches offer remarkable beauty where soft white sand meets pristine blue waters. This tranquil stretch of the Sri Lankan coast gives visitors a welcome escape from crowded beaches, providing an authentic tropical experience. Early morning hours reveal expansive golden shores, with only local fishermen working quietly on their nets. Swimming conditions remain safe throughout the year, with gentle waves that make it perfect for families with children.
The area's most captivating feature is the traditional stilt fishermen, balanced skillfully on wooden poles like seasoned performers. These talented artisans enjoy sharing stories of their daily catches with interested visitors. The local dining scene offers excellent fresh seafood, with beachside establishments serving fish caught that same morning alongside coconut water served directly from the shell. When evening arrives, Ahangama becomes a spectacular sunset viewing destination, all enjoyed without the heavy crowds found at other popular beaches.
Destinations to Discover
The island spirit serenades us here and beckons you to wander within our dreamlike destinations. With so much to explore, there is no limit to what you might discover.

Discover the magic of Sri Lanka's coast on our Sri Lanka cycle tours with Mosvold Boutique Hotels. Ride easy paths through green rice fields, coconut groves, and quiet fishing spots. See how locals live as you pedal through small villages. Our friendly guides know all the best places and stories, making this cycling experience both fun and eye-opening. ## Madol Duwa Boat Safari, Sri Lanka | Mosvold Villa

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/mosvold-villa/experiences/madol-duwa-boat-safari/

> Enjoy your madol duwa boat safari through mangroves and calm waters, perfect for nature lovers and explorers in Sri Lanka. Explore now!

Cinnamon Tour
The Cinnamon experience in Koggala lets you explore the ancient spice in an experiential journey. Take the trails of the planter and be treated to a tea and lunch inspired by Cinnamon.

scroll down

Experience
- Distance from hotel : 2km / 5-10 mins
- Duration : 3 hours
- Participant Capacity : 6 -20
2km/5-10 mins
Explore the peaceful backwaters of southern Sri Lanka on a Madol Duwa boat safari just 15 minutes from our hotel. Drift past lush mangroves where monkeys play and colorful birds nest among the branches. Your guide points out wildlife and shares stories from the famous novel set in this area. The trip to Madol Duwa island offers a gentle escape into the setting that inspired one of Sri Lanka's best-loved books. Watch local fishermen cast their nets as they have for hundreds of years, their boats drifting silently on the calm water.
Walk on the island that captured writer Martin Wickramasinghe's heart and became home to his most famous story. Follow the same paths as the book's young hero and see village life that continues much as it was described. Enjoy fresh king coconut water in the shade of tall palms during your visit. The Madol Duwa boat safari brings together Sri Lanka's rich culture and natural beauty. This short morning trip gives you great photos and deeper insights into local life. It's a perfect way to step into the pages of a classic Sri Lankan tale.
Destinations to Discover
The island spirit serenades us here and beckons you to wander within our dreamlike destinations. With so much to explore, there is no limit to what you might discover.

## Cultural Tours Sri Lanka | Monastery & Temple Walks | Mosvold Villa

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/mosvold-villa/experiences/monastery-temple-walks/

> Experience some of the best cultural attractions with Mosvold, as you explore ancient temples, monasteries, and vibrant traditions. Explore now!

Cinnamon Tour
The Cinnamon experience in Koggala lets you explore the ancient spice in an experiential journey. Take the trails of the planter and be treated to a tea and lunch inspired by Cinnamon.

scroll down

Experience
- Distance from Hotel : 2km-10km /5 minutes – 15 minutes
- Duration : 3-4 Hours
- Participant Capacity : Max 8 guests
Our Sri Lankan Cultural Tour takes on spiritual meaning with our guided Monastery Walks where you traverse through ancient Buddhist temples nestled in the southern hills near Ahangama. Follow peaceful paths past meditation caves, rocky boulders, and old stupas where monks have lived in quiet simplicity for centuries. Your guide shares stories of Buddha's teachings and how they shape daily life on the island. Listen to temple bells and rustling leaves that create a calming rhythm as you walk. The dense forest setting offers a soulful break from beach adventures.
Join in a short meditation session or watch as locals bring colorful offerings to shrines. You might see monks in bright orange robes going about their daily tasks or receive a blessing along your journey. These walks invite you to slow down and connect with traditions thousands of years old. The experience lasts 3-4 hours, visiting 2-3 monasteries within 30 minutes of our hotels. Groups are kept small (maximum 8 people) to preserve the peaceful atmosphere. Come dressed modestly with shoulders and knees covered to show respect.
Destinations to Discover
The island spirit serenades us here and beckons you to wander within our dreamlike destinations. With so much to explore, there is no limit to what you might discover.

## Stilt Fishing in Galle, Sri Lanka | Mosvold Villa

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/mosvold-villa/experiences/stilt-fishing/

> Experience the ancient art of stilt fishing in Galle. Watch the fishermen in action or try it for yourself. Book now with Mosvold Villa!

Cinnamon Tour
The Cinnamon experience in Koggala lets you explore the ancient spice in an experiential journey. Take the trails of the planter and be treated to a tea and lunch inspired by Cinnamon.

scroll down

Experience
- Distance from Hotel : 3-8 km / 5-20 mins
- Duration : 2 hours
- Participant Capacity : Maximum 10 visitors per session to ensure quality interaction with the local fishermen.
Ever seen those iconic photos of fishermen perched on wooden poles above the water? That's stilt fishing in Sri Lanka, a centuries-old tradition you can witness firsthand near our property! These skilled local fishermen balance expertly on narrow stilts planted into the coral reef, patiently waiting to catch fish using just a rod and line. On this experience, you'll watch this fascinating practice during the golden hour when the light is perfect and the fishing is most active. It's a photographer's dream and a genuine glimpse into Sri Lanka's coastal heritage.
Want to try something truly unique? At select times, visitors can even attempt stilt fishing in Galle under the guidance of experienced fishermen! You'll gain appreciation for the incredible balance and patience required while learning about how this traditional practice has adapted to modern times. The local fishermen share stories about their craft while demonstrating techniques passed down through generations. This respectful cultural exchange supports local communities while providing an unforgettable connection to Sri Lanka's maritime traditions.
Destinations to Discover
The island spirit serenades us here and beckons you to wander within our dreamlike destinations. With so much to explore, there is no limit to what you might discover.

## Kabalana Beach Surf Point, Sri Lanka | Mosvold Villa

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/mosvold-villa/experiences/surfing-at-kabalana/

> Catch the perfect wave at Kabalana Surf Point, Sri Lanka’s top A-frame reef break, with Mosvold Villa as your ideal stay. Book now!

Ride the waves at Kabalana Beach surf, a gem on Sri Lanka's south coast. This famous Kabalana surf spot offers perfect breaks for all skill levels. Catch gentle waves near shore if you're learning, or paddle out to the reef for bigger thrills. The clear blue water stays warm year-round, and the beach is just steps from Mosvold Villa. Morning sessions reward early risers with smooth conditions and peaceful moments on the water.
After your time at Kabalana surf point, the beach life continues. Grab fresh fruit from local vendors or sip coconut water under swaying palms. Watch the sunset paint the sky as surfers catch their last waves of the day. The friendly crowd shares a love for the ocean and island pace. Kabalana's consistent waves and laid-back vibe make every day special whether you're here for a week or a month. This is Sri Lankan surfing at its best.

## Tea Plantation Tours in Ahangama, Sri Lanka | Mosvold Villa

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/mosvold-villa/experiences/tea-plantation-visit/

> Explore the Handunugoda Tea Estate near Mosvold Villa and enjoy exceptional Ceylon Tea. Book now for a unique experience!

Cinnamon Tour
The Cinnamon experience in Koggala lets you explore the ancient spice in an experiential journey. Take the trails of the planter and be treated to a tea and lunch inspired by Cinnamon.

scroll down

Experience
- Distance from Hotel : 6.2 KM /10 mins
- Duration : 4 hours
- Participant Capacity : 1-12 visitors
Ever dreamed of seeing where your morning cup of tea begins its journey? The tea plantation tour of Sri Lanka is a must-do experience that'll leave you amazed! Wander through stunning emerald hillsides where expert tea pluckers skillfully gather leaves for Ceylon's famous teas. You'll love exploring the virgin white tea plantation, Ahangama, where workers actually wear gloves to avoid touching the delicate leaves! The Ahangama tea estate tour shows you how they've perfected sustainable farming over generations. With cool mountain breezes and jaw-dropping views, it's the perfect escape from the beach heat.
Ready for the best part? Your tea estate tour continues to the factory, where you'll see exactly how those green leaves become the perfect cuppa! A tea expert will show you how to properly taste different varieties – you'll never look at tea the same way again! Then relax at the charming plantation café with freshly brewed tea and local sweets while soaking in those magnificent hillside views. The whole experience connects you to a tradition that's been central to Sri Lankan life for centuries. Who knew learning about tea could be this fun?
Destinations to Discover
The island spirit serenades us here and beckons you to wander within our dreamlike destinations. With so much to explore, there is no limit to what you might discover.

## Visit Galle Turtle Hatchery | Mosvold Villa

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/mosvold-villa/experiences/turtle-hatchery-ahangama/

> Get close to hatchling turtles at the Habaraduwa Turtle Hatchery in Galle and support sea turtle conservation efforts. Stay at Mosvold Villa and book now!

Cinnamon Tour
The Cinnamon experience in Koggala lets you explore the ancient spice in an experiential journey. Take the trails of the planter and be treated to a tea and lunch inspired by Cinnamon.

scroll down

Experience
- Distance from hotel : 5-6 km / 5-10 mins
Sri Lanka is home to seven types of sea turtles; some of them are endangered.
Turtle Hatcheries around the coast play a vital role in protecting endangered sea turtles, providing a safe haven for their eggs and ensuring that hatchlings make it to the ocean.
As a visitor, you can learn about the life cycle of sea turtles, witness the care taken in incubating their eggs, and even watch baby turtles being released into the sea—a truly magical sight.
Many hatcheries also rehabilitate injured turtles, showcasing the local commitment to preserving marine life. This visit not only offers a close encounter with these gentle creatures but also supports conservation efforts, making it both a meaningful and memorable part of your journey.
Speak to our concierge and we can arrange a visit to the nearby turtle hatcheries.
Destinations to Discover
The island spirit serenades us here and beckons you to wander within our dreamlike destinations. With so much to explore, there is no limit to what you might discover.

## Galle Fort Attractions & Tours, Sri Lanka | Mosvold Villa

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/mosvold-villa/experiences/visit-galle-fort/

> Explore Galle Fort rich history, stunning lighthouse, and cultural treasures, all while staying at Mosvold Villa. A must-visit for history and beach lovers. Book now!

Cinnamon Tour
The Cinnamon experience in Koggala lets you explore the ancient spice in an experiential journey. Take the trails of the planter and be treated to a tea and lunch inspired by Cinnamon.

scroll down

Experience
- Distance from Hotel : 18.4 KM /20 mins
- Duration : One day
- Participant Capacity : Any
Travel back in time as you wander in the old city of Galle. With its cobble-stoned streets lined with old colonial houses and buildings, Galle Fort is a must-see location when travelling in Sri Lanka. Marked as a UNESCO World Heritage Site, Galle Fort is a reminder of a time by gone. The town is a melting pot of culture, cuisine, people, creativity and every day life. While it is a tourist hotspot, people still live here and go about their daly life: the old town has everything i.e. offices, courts, cafes, gem & antique shoes, clothing stores, restaurants, hotels, museums, temples, churches and so much more.
You can walk around the whole town within a day, but if you like, you can always come back to check out specific places or things to do. You can start the day by meandering within the Fort, checking out whatever catches your eye, having some amazing food, catching the sunset over the rampart and then heading back.
Destinations to Discover
The island spirit serenades us here and beckons you to wander within our dreamlike destinations. With so much to explore, there is no limit to what you might discover.

## Whale and Dolphin Watching in Sri Lanka | Mosvold Villa

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/mosvold-villa/experiences/whale-and-dolphin-watching/

> Witness blue whales and dolphins in their natural habitat on an unforgettable tour, with Mosvold Villa as your base. Book now for an amazing experience in Sri Lanka!

Cinnamon Tour
The Cinnamon experience in Koggala lets you explore the ancient spice in an experiential journey. Take the trails of the planter and be treated to a tea and lunch inspired by Cinnamon.

scroll down

Experience
- Distance from Hotel : 17 km / 15-20 mins
- Duration : 1-4 hours
- Participant Capacity : 10-100 or more
The waters off the coast of Mirissa and other southern towns are renowned for their biodiversity, offering a chance to spot blue whales—the largest animals on Earth—alongside playful pods of dolphins and other species like sperm whales, orcas, pilot whales, humpback whales, dolphins, flying fish, turtles, manta rays and whale sharks in their natural habitat.
The best time for this adventure is between November and April, when the seas are calm and marine activity is at its peak. The sight of these majestic creatures breaching or gliding through the azure waters is awe-inspiring, making this eco-adventure a highlight for wildlife enthusiasts, photographers, and ocean lovers.
Speak to our concierge and we can arrange your whale-watching experience during your stay with us.
Destinations to Discover
The island spirit serenades us here and beckons you to wander within our dreamlike destinations. With so much to explore, there is no limit to what you might discover.

## Beachfront Luxury Villas in South Sri Lanka | Mosvold Villa

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/mosvold-villa/location/

> Escape to beachfront luxury in the South of Sri Lanka at Mosvold Villa. Book your stay in our exclusive villas today!

Valid till 14th Nov 2025
Head Start Early Bird Offer – Non Sri Lankans
Book 90 or more days in advance from the date of arrival and enjoy 15% discount

scroll down

Escape into the Enchanting Embrace of
Welcome to your tropical paradise.
Here in Ahangama, where the sand shines golden and the ocean reminds us of the beauty of life, you are invited to take some time off.
There is no rushing in this part of the world. Only the swaying of coconut trees and the sound of the sea gently brushing over our bare feet.
On this side of the island, the sun celebrates each day by dancing in the evening sky, which explodes into hues of orange, purple and pink.

Our Location
Travelling from the airport or Colombo is scenic and convenient. Public and private buses, trains, private transport, and our shuttle service will help you get it.

Create unforgettable memories with our exclusive offers! Don't miss the chance to indulge in a remarkable stay that you’ll cherish forever. Grab these limited-time deals and experience luxury beach gateaway!

## Luxury Beach Villas in Sri Lanka | Ahangama | Mosvold Villa

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/mosvold-villa/suites-and-rooms-at-mosvold-villa/

> Experience luxury by the Indian Ocean at Mosvold Villa, where stunning rooms, peaceful views, and unique island crafts await. Book now!

Breathtaking ocean views, modern amenities and plush interiors are ideal for a tropical beachside getaway.

![Suites and Rooms at Mosvold Villa](https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/mosvold-villa/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2025/04/Suites-and-Rooms-at-Mosvold-Villa-1920x630-1.jpg)
scroll down

Immerse Yourself in the Indian Ocean
Looking on to a breathtaking uninterrupted expanse of the Indian Ocean our boutique hotel invites you to indulge in the luxury of 16 meticulously crafted rooms and suites, each offering a relaxing and unforgettable escape.
Through our thoughtful design, we celebrate our little island and the intricate crafts that are unique to our people.
Ocean-inspired batik prints on linen, preserved palm leaves on intricate wood designs, vintage cane furnishings and ocean-themed artistry are a tribute to our heritage.
Breathtaking ocean views, modern amenities and plush interiors are ideal for a tropical beachside getaway.

Comfortable and elegant spaces, fun activities and mesmerising nature scenes await you and your family.

Step into an expansive suite with a private pool to compliment a relaxed setting surrounded by lush beauty and sweeping oceanfront views.

Serene garden views, elegant designs and premium amenities for a holiday of making memories.

# Sundara (Balapitiya)

## Balapitiya Boutique Hotel | Beach Hotels in Sri Lanka | Sundara

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/sundara/

> Escape to Sundara in Balapitiya. Relax in a beachfront haven offering luxury and tranquillity. Experience unmatched hospitality. Book now!

A seamless fusion of comfort and sophistication

![Sundara by Mosvold](https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/sundara/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/06/sundara-by-mosvold-1920x1000-2.jpg)
scroll down

Sundara By Mosvold
Wander into picturesque sceneries right out of a vintage postcard. Built to resemble a luxurious bungalow, Sundara exudes an old-world charm. True to its Sanskrit meaning, Sundara offers a beautiful journey back in time. From the minute you arrive, you know you are somewhere exceptional.
Book Directly
No booking fee
Best deals guaranteed
No hidden costs
Transform Your Stay with Our
With eight exquisite rooms and villas overlooking the shimmering waters of the Indian Ocean, Sundara combines old-world charm with new-world comfort.
Experience world-renowned Sri Lankan hospitality in our spacious staycation designed for comfort and relaxation.

Unforgettable Experiences Await
Balapitiya is a place that rekindles the senses. Connect with the culture, community, and nature our property is home to an immersive experience.

Discover Special Deals
Unlock our experiences with these curated packages that add extra value to your stay at Sundara by Mosvold

Insights & Inspiration
Browse our blog to take a sneak peek into our destinations. We have things to do, sights to see, people to meet, food to taste—we hardly ever run out of stories to tell.

## Beach Restaurants in Balapitiya, Sri Lanka | Sundara

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/sundara/dining/

> Savor exceptional dining with stunning beach views at Sundara's restaurant. Book now for a memorable seaside experience!

Sip & Slide Beverage Menu
Pull up a chair where the sun meets the sea at our bar. Try your favourite drinks with a twist of Sri Lankan spice.

scroll down

The Flavours of Our Heritage
Food is our love language. When you think of Sri Lankan hospitality, you immediately think of the warmth, care and nourishment that you get from the food that we lovingly prepare.
“Home-cooked meals” have a special place in our hearts. No matter where we go, food always connects us back to home. Whether it is nourishment from the land or the sea, coastal areas of Sri Lanka are gastronomical adventures waiting to be had.
Another Kind of Depth
Dine al fresco under the stars with the sound of waves as your backdrop, or enjoy a cosy indoor setting with panoramic ocean views.
Savour the Southern Shore
Relish in the diverse flavours of the Sri Lankan South, fostering an atmosphere for guests to convene and partake in the authentic spirit of the island.

## Madu Ganga Boat Trips & Day Packages in Sri Lanka | Sundara

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/sundara/experiences/a-boat-ride-on-the-madhu-river/

> Experience a thrilling river safari and explore Madu Ganga with Sundara’s exclusive boat trip packages. Book now!

Wildlife Safari in Yala
Track elusive leopards and witness elephants roaming freely in Sri Lanka’s premier wildlife sanctuary.

scroll down

Experience
- Distance from Hotel : 2 km /10 mins
- Duration : 4 hours
- Participant Capacity : 10-20
Step onto a boat and escape into the serene tranquility of the Madhu River. As you cruise through this lush mangrove ecosystem, you'll encounter a fascinating array of wildlife, including colorful birds, monitor lizards, and even playful monkeys along the riverbanks. The journey takes you past small islands, each with its own charm—like the one housing a centuries-old Buddhist temple.
You'll also get a chance to see traditional cinnamon peeling and fishing methods, offering a glimpse into the local way of life. The peaceful ambiance, combined with the vibrant natural surroundings, makes a Madhu River boat ride an unforgettable experience for nature lovers and adventurers alike.
Destinations to Discover
The island spirit serenades us here and beckons you to wander within our dreamlike destinations. With so much to explore, there is no limit to what you might discover.

## Ambalangoda Mask Museum & Traditional Arts in Sri Lanka | Sundara

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/sundara/experiences/ambalangoda-ariyapala-mask-museum/

> Explore Sri Lanka's traditional masks and crafts at the Ambalangoda Mask Museum with Sundara. Immerse yourself in culture and art on your visit! Book now!

Wildlife Safari in Yala
Track elusive leopards and witness elephants roaming freely in Sri Lanka’s premier wildlife sanctuary.

scroll down

Experience
- Distance from Hotel : 6.5 km /10 mins
- Duration : 2 hours
- Participant Capacity : Max 12
Journey 25 minutes from Sundara by Mosvold to explore one of the traditional arts and crafts of Sri Lanka at the renowned Ambalangoda Mask Museum. Watch skilled artisans carve and paint vibrant masks used in ancient healing rituals and folk performances. The museum houses rare examples dating back centuries, each telling stories of demons and deities that have shaped local culture. Return to Sundara's peaceful gardens with newfound appreciation for these intricate creations, perhaps with a small treasure from the workshop.
The traditional Sri Lankan masks come alive as museum guides demonstrate how dancers transform when wearing these powerful faces. Family techniques passed through generations are preserved here, where you can try your hand at basic painting techniques. The Ambalangoda Mask Museum has special demonstrations of the thunderous devil dances these masks were designed for. Afterward, enjoy a scenic coastal drive back to Sundara, where the contrast between ancient tradition and modern comfort creates the perfect balance.
Destinations to Discover
The island spirit serenades us here and beckons you to wander within our dreamlike destinations. With so much to explore, there is no limit to what you might discover.

## Beach Camping Balapitiya | Balapitiya Beach Sri lanka | Sundara

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/sundara/experiences/beaches-balapitiya/

> Enjoy beach camping Balapitiya, sleep under the stars, relax by the sea, and take in the peaceful coastal vibes of Sri Lanka. Explore now!

Wildlife Safari in Yala
Track elusive leopards and witness elephants roaming freely in Sri Lanka’s premier wildlife sanctuary.

scroll down

Experience
The sea surrounds you here. Each new beach is as stunning as the one before. Within half an hour of our property, you can visit Kabalana, Hikkaduwa, Bentota, Narigama and Balapitiya Beaches.
- Distance from Hotel : Walking Distance/ 5 mins
- Duration : 2-4 hours
- Participant Capacity : Any
Balapitiya Beach in Sri Lanka is truly one of those places that makes you wonder why more people don't visit it. This stretch of coastline feels almost private, with soft golden sand that's perfect for long walks and clear waters that stay surprisingly calm most days. You'll find coconut trees providing natural shade, and the whole area has this relaxed vibe that's hard to find at busier beaches. It's the kind of locale where you can actually hear the waves instead of crowds.
The best time for swimming is definitely morning or late afternoon when the water's at its calmest, especially up north near where the river meets the sea. The safe swimming time period is between December and March. If you're staying at Sundara By Mosvold Hotel, you can easily hop over to check out the Madu River boat safari or visit the Sundara Turtle Hatchery - both are worth the trip and make for a pretty amazing day exploring this part of Sri Lanka's coast.
Destinations to Discover
The island spirit serenades us here and beckons you to wander within our dreamlike destinations. With so much to explore, there is no limit to what you might discover.

## Geoffrey Bawa | Lunuganga Bentota, Sri Lanka | Sundara

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/sundara/experiences/lunuganga-estate-by-geoffrey-bawa/

> Wander through lunuganga bentota, the serene garden estate of Geoffrey Bawa—Sri Lanka’s iconic architect and visionary. A timeless escape. Explore now!

Wildlife Safari in Yala
Track elusive leopards and witness elephants roaming freely in Sri Lanka’s premier wildlife sanctuary.

scroll down

Experience
- Distance from Hotel : 18 km / 15mins
- Duration : 2.5 hours
- Participant Capacity : Max 8 People
Venture just 20 minutes from Sundara by Mosvold to step into Geoffrey Bawa's masterpiece hidden among cinnamon groves. Lunuganga Bentota, Sri Lanka offers a glimpse into the mind of Asia's architectural genius through winding paths where each turn reveals another perfectly framed view. Stone steps lead to secret gardens where ancient trees bend over reflective pools. Birds call from canopies while you discover how Bawa transformed a cinnamon estate into living art that changes with each season and hour.
From the main terrace, the Lunuganga lake view in Bentota stretches before you – an expanse of blue rimmed by distant temples and swaying palms. Your guide shares stories of Geoffrey Bawa, Sri Lanka, revealing how this country lawyer became a visionary who forever changed tropical design. Touch the weathered stones he personally placed, rest on benches positioned for contemplation, and understand why artists still make pilgrimages here. Return to Sundara's own thoughtful design, now seeing with new eyes how Bawa's influence shapes your beachside haven.
Destinations to Discover
The island spirit serenades us here and beckons you to wander within our dreamlike destinations. With so much to explore, there is no limit to what you might discover.

## Sunandarama Maha Viharaya | Ancient Temples in Sri Lanka | Sundara

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/sundara/experiences/sundarama-maha-viharaya/

> Discover the spiritual beauty of ancient temples in Sri Lanka with Sundara—uncover history, architecture, and sacred traditions on your journey. Explore now!

Wildlife Safari in Yala
Track elusive leopards and witness elephants roaming freely in Sri Lanka’s premier wildlife sanctuary.

scroll down

Experience
- Distance from Hotel : 8 km /10-15 mins
- Duration : 4 hours
- Participant Capacity : Max 15
Take a quick five-minute drive from Sundara by Mosvold to visit Sundarama* Maha Viharaya. This lovely temple has big Buddha statues, pretty carvings, and old wall paintings. It’s one of the best ancient temples in Sri Lanka that most tourists miss. Tall trees give shade as local people come to pray. Here you can see how Buddhism has shaped our island’s way of life for many years. The friendly temple keeper often shares simple stories about the temple’s past. Kids love to look at the colorful flags and flowers that people bring as gifts.
The temple sits in a green, peaceful spot that helps you relax and feel calm. Soft bells and sweet smells fill the air. Monks wear bright orange robes as they walk around the open spaces. Unlike busy ancient temples in Sri Lanka, this quiet place feels real and special. Come in the morning to see daily offerings, or visit at sunset when small lamps make the temple glow with warm light. Our hotel staff can help arrange your visit and tell you about temple rules. Many guests say this temple visit was the best part of their stay. Bring your camera, but ask before taking photos of monks or people who are praying.
Destinations to Discover
The island spirit serenades us here and beckons you to wander within our dreamlike destinations. With so much to explore, there is no limit to what you might discover.

## Kosgoda Turtle Conservation Project | Turtles Watching in Sri Lanka | Sundara

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/sundara/experiences/turtle-conservation-project/

> Explore the Kosgoda Turtle Conservation Project and enjoy turtle watching in Sri Lanka. Book your experience now for a unique wildlife adventure at Sundara!

Wildlife Safari in Yala
Track elusive leopards and witness elephants roaming freely in Sri Lanka’s premier wildlife sanctuary.

scroll down

Experience
- Distance from Hotel : 9 km /15 mins
- Duration : 3 hours
- Participant Capacity : Max 15 people
Witness nature's ancient mariners up close at Sundara by Mosvold's exclusive turtle-watching experience in Sri Lanka. The adventure unfolds at dusk or dawn when tiny hatchlings make their determined dash to the sea. Guests gather in small groups along moonlit beaches, watching in hushed awe as these perfect miniatures, no bigger than poker chips, navigate past unnatural obstacles – like artificial lights and beach chairs. The staff from the Kosgoda turtle conservation project share stories of each species' unique challenges while guiding visitors to observe them respectfully.
Morning visits to the Kosgoda turtle sanctuary reveal the other side of conservation – injured adults receiving care in recovery pools. These magnificent creatures, some weighing over 300 pounds, bear propeller scars and fishing line injuries that tell silent stories of human impact. Sundara by Mosvold guests help prepare special meals for these patients and assist in releasing healthy hatchlings.
Children particularly connect with naming the baby turtles they help release, fostering memories and nurturing a new generation of ocean guardians – a perfect blend of education and meaningful travel.
Destinations to Discover
The island spirit serenades us here and beckons you to wander within our dreamlike destinations. With so much to explore, there is no limit to what you might discover.

## Walking Tours & Day Trips in Galle Fort, Sri Lanka | Sundara

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/sundara/experiences/visit-galle-fort/

> Discover the rich history and charm of Galle Fort on an unforgettable walking tour with Sundara. Book your Galle day trip now for an experience like no other!

Wildlife Safari in Yala
Track elusive leopards and witness elephants roaming freely in Sri Lanka’s premier wildlife sanctuary.

scroll down

Experience
- Distance from Hotel : 39 km / 40 mins
- Duration : One day
- Participant Capacity : Any
Galle Fort, a UNESCO World Heritage Site, is just 39 km from Sundara by Mosvold. Originally built by Portuguese explorers in the 16th century and later expanded by Dutch colonists, this remarkably preserved coastal fortress showcases a fascinating blend of European and South Asian architectural styles. Wander cobblestone lanes lined with colonial buildings and visit iconic Galle Fort attractions, including the historic Galle Fort Lighthouse, Old Dutch Hospital, and Maritime Museum. Walk along ancient ramparts for breathtaking ocean views.
Browse unique local artwork at beloved shops like Barefoot and Stick No Bills, then savor delicious meals at Church Street Social or artisanal gelato from Isle of Gelato. Join our guided Galle Fort tours and explore hidden corners on foot or by bicycle at your own comfortable pace. Our knowledgeable staff will help arrange your ideal visit to this enchanting destination where colonial history meets vibrant island culture. This half-day adventure, combining ancient stones, sea breezes, and living heritage, will become a highlight of your Sri Lankan getaway.
Destinations to Discover
The island spirit serenades us here and beckons you to wander within our dreamlike destinations. With so much to explore, there is no limit to what you might discover.

Feel the rush of warm wind in your hair while you visit Bentota water sports. Just 15 minutes from Sundara by Mosvold, the plethora of water sports available in Bentota turn ordinary days into stories worth telling. Carved boards slice through waves as local instructors teach you to ride the ocean's rhythm.
Morning reveals glassy waters perfect for wakeboarding – your falls cushioned by the warm sea before success comes with whoops of triumph. By afternoon, when sea breezes pick up, colorful sails dot the horizon as windsurfers dance with invisible currents. Return salt-crusted and smiling to Sundara's shade, where cold king coconuts await and staff who remember your name ask about your adventures while the day's excitement settles into pleasant muscle fatigue.

## Yala National Park Safari | Wildlife Safari in Sri Lanka | Sundara

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/sundara/experiences/wildlife-safari-in-yala/

> Experience the thrill of a Yala National Park Safari with Sundara, enjoy amazing close-up wildlife moments and take in the peaceful beauty of the wild.Explore now!

Turtle Conservation Project
Release baby turtles into moonlit waves and nurture injured adults back to health.

scroll down

Experience
- Distance from Hotel : 180km/2.5 hours
- Duration : 1 day
- Participant Capacity : 6 per jeep
Guests at Sundara by Mosvold shouldn't miss exploring Yala National Park, home to one of the world's highest leopard populations. The hotel arranges expert-guided Yala wildlife safaris that depart before dawn when animals are most active. Bumping along dusty tracks in open-top jeeps, visitors often spot elephants tearing branches from trees, crocodiles lurking in pools, and if luck smiles, the park's elusive leopards draped over rocky outcrops. The guides – many who've spent decades tracking these animals – know exactly which watering holes attract wildlife during dry spells and which hidden corners might reveal a sloth bear digging for termites.
The afternoon brings different Sri Lankan wildlife safari experiences as the blazing sun softens and creatures emerge from hideaways. Peacocks display their magnificent feathers while monkeys chatter overhead. Safaris at the Yala National Park continue until dusk, when the landscape transforms with golden light and predators become more active. Halfway through, the driver will find a scenic spot where you can enjoy refreshments prepared by Sundara's kitchen. Many guests return still buzzing about a mother elephant shepherding her calf or a majestic leopard that locked eyes with them – the kind of authentic wilderness encounters that become treasured vacation memories. The park's wildlife provides unforgettable photography opportunities in this untamed wilderness.
Destinations to Discover
The island spirit serenades us here and beckons you to wander within our dreamlike destinations. With so much to explore, there is no limit to what you might discover.

At Sundara by Mosvold's luxury yoga retreat in Sri Lanka, the morning light creates a golden glow that makes everything feel possible - no exaggeration needed. The yoga sessions welcome everyone – from those who have no clue what a downward dog is, to those who can bend like pretzels. The instructors monitor and guide guests and their movements where required, all to the the soundtrack of waves hitting sand.
Then comes the best part – heading to the Sundara spa still in that post-yoga calm. The treatments offered by experienced therapists at Mosvold go beyond traditional offerings. Incredible local ingredients – herbs, oils and rare clays are used by them for a truly authentic experience. Sundara by Mosvold is the real deal – wellness without the pretense.

## Boutique Hotels in Balapitiya, Sri Lanka South Coast | Sundara

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/sundara/location/

> Escape to Sundara on Sri Lanka’s south coast. Easy access from Colombo makes it the perfect luxury retreat. Book now!

Valid till 30th Nov 2025.
Sunset Lovers’ Honeymoon Offer | Non-Sri Lankans
Celebrate your honeymoon in Luxury at Sundara by Mosvold.

scroll down

Escape into Eternal Elegance at
Welcome to your seaside oasis.
Here in Balapitiya, where the ocean breeze sings the songs of sea people and the coconut trees sway to the lull of Mother Earth, you are invited to unplug, rewind and rediscover.
Ours is an island of slowing down. “Island mentality” as we call it. It is a way of life; the movement of nature where nothing or no one rushes, but everything happens in time.
Here, where the people move with the ocean, we celebrate each day for just arriving.
Pause with us.
Our Location
Travelling from the airport or Colombo is scenic and convenient. Distance from Colombo to Balapitiya is about 100 km (roughly 2 hours)
Public and private buses, trains, private transport and our shuttle service will help you get it.

Create unforgettable memories with our exclusive offers! Don't miss the chance to indulge in a remarkable stay that you’ll cherish forever.

## Luxury Beach Accommodation in Sri Lanka | Balapitiya | Sundara

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/sundara/suites-and-rooms-at-sundara/

> Stay at Sundara, a colonial-style boutique hotel with 8 exquisite rooms, private villas, and vintage charm by the ocean. Book now!

A seamless fusion of comfort and sophistication

![Suites and Rooms at Sundara](https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/sundara/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/05/Rooms-at-Sundara-1920x630-1.jpg)
scroll down

Surround Yourself with Vintage Charm
Hidden along the Balapitiya coastal stretch, our oceanfront boutique hotel features 8 exquisite rooms and villas including a two-bedroomed family villa with a private pool and a beach villa.
Inspired by our ancestors' way of life, we built a luxury colonial-style bungalow that combines old-world charm with a modern twist.
With this dreamscape, we celebrate our ancestors, the way they lived in harmony with our island, and the intricate crafts that are unique to our people.
Ocean-inspired batik prints on linen, preserved palm leaves on intricate wood designs, vintage cane furnishings and ocean-themed artistry are a tribute to our heritage.
A seamless fusion of comfort and sophistication

Secluded luxury overlooking the ocean

Thoughtfully curated interiors with stunning sea views

Unmatched tranquillity in a spacious private villa with a private pool

Peaceful garden views complement a contemporary design

## 10 reasons to visit Sri lanka in 2024

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/blog/10-reasons-to-visit-sri-lanka-in-2024/

> Discover 10 compelling reasons to visit Sri Lanka in 2024, from beaches and wildlife to culture, food, and adventure. Explore more.

![Coconut Tree Hill in Mirissa](https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/03/10-reasons-to-visit-Sri-lanka-in-2024-1920x600-2.jpg)
scroll down

10 reasons to visit Sri lanka in 2024
June 1, 2024

With political protests and unrest, Sri Lanka’s tourism took a great downfall in late 2022. However, with government governance and efforts, the tourism industry recovered and Sri Lanka has seen a more than 3 % increase in visitor arrivals. The new government and officials are doing their utmost to increase visitor arrivals to Sri Lanka to revive the economy in 2024. This can be done by uplifting the hotel industry and introducing distinctive deals and services. Sri Lanka expects a great spike in tourism arrivals in 2024 and the next hopefully. Sri Lanka, a serene and lush destination has recently gained attention and made numerous “Top Travel Destinations” lists. Due to its diverse culture, cuisine, sports activities, beaches and it’s amazing accommodations, there are a plethora of opulent hotels popping up to accommodate the close to 2 million visitors who visit the island’s sandy shores every year.
Do you want to visit Sri Lanka? This is exactly why! Every passionate globetrotter can find something to enjoy in Sri Lanka! It is where waterfalls run and elephants roam. Tea plants flourish and beaches are plentiful. This beautiful island is home to a diverse range of sacred places, historic cities, and lush tea plantations. It nurtures different languages, ethnic groups, and cultural traditions. Whether you are traveling with a group of friends or family, on a group tour, or on a private vacation, this splendid country is like no other! Here are 10 reasons to visit Sri Lanka in 2024!
Sri Lanka is paradise on Earth! With its unique fauna and wildlife, it has the best nature reserves and wildlife parks that you cannot miss! With acres of lush forests and grasslands, Yala, Wasgamuwa, and Udawalawe are the most famous wildlife reserves. Yala is well-known for having a high leopard population, Wasgamuwa for seeing sloth bears, and Udawalawe for wild elephants. You can find a few camping areas and safari rides that let visitors stay close to a variety of creatures, including spotted deer, wild boar, crocodiles, monkeys, and plentiful birdlife. Horton Plains is surrounded by rolling hills and thick montane forests in the highlands. It is known as one of Sri Lanka’s World Heritage sites and is a stunning location to explore and observe wildlife, such as the endangered sambar deer and leopards of Sri Lanka.
There’s nothing like some beach time to make a perfect vacation. Sri Lanka has miles of beautiful, unspoiled beaches and turquoise waters. And, there’s a beach for every type of traveler! The beaches on the south coast like Mirissa are quiet and laidback, while the beaches in Unawatuna are crowded and buzzing with parties and nightlife activities. Bentota has the best beaches for water activities, like kitesurfing. Some of the world’s most stunning beaches can be found in Sri Lanka. Some of these beaches are ideal for snorkeling and surfing, while the majority are ideal for sunbathing and total relaxation. The beaches of Sri Lanka are one of the many reasons for tourists to visit! So many of the country’s hotels and resorts are situated there. A few coastal towns have a sense of history or vibrant nightlife! While soaking up some sun, sand, and waves, you can visit turtle hatcheries, batik stores, and collect trendy souvenirs for your loved ones!
Sri Lanka stands out as one of the top locations for intermediate and beginning surfers. This is one of the best 10 reasons to visit Sri Lanka in 2024. As surfing has become a highly trending activity, You can surf in one of Sri Lanka’s best beaches to unleash your grappling surfing energy! Whether you are a beginner or a pro, Sri Lanka is a great place to learn to surf or to advance to more difficult waves. Due to the range of waves and the presence of some truly excellent surf instructors, surfing is accessible to surfers of all skill levels here. Make sure you visit Hiriketiya Beach, Arugam Bay, and Ahangama Bay for a one-of-a-kind surfing experience!
Sri Lanka is a fantastic vacation spot for yogis! Although yoga has its roots in India, Sri Lankan society has always embraced this age-old spiritual discipline. Sri Lanka is one of the best countries in Asia to perfect your practice, regardless of how experienced you are with yoga. While in Mirissa, think about participating in one of Soul Yoga’s weekend retreats for a more complete yoga experience. You can go through a journey of physical yoga, breathing exercises, and meditation with a focus on rest, relaxation, and reflection. This is a lovely way to spend a weekend in Mirissa, whether you want to hone your practice or take your first steps on the path to becoming a yogi!
You can experience a range of customized Ayurveda treatments that come along with yoga, meditation, and other spiritual practices that are combined with 30-day or longer continuous treatment programs. You can relax and enjoy the best services in opulent Ayurvedic resorts and medical facilities. You are in excellent hands with a team of highly experienced professionals giving expert help! These Ayurvedic therapies are properly prepared and carried out in Sri Lanka under the direct supervision of our licensed physicians and spa experts.
Sri Lanka’s ecotourism sector is booming! It is one of the top 10 reasons to visit Sri Lanka in 2024! Both local and foreign tourists are drawn to the lush tropical forests, which protect a variety of animal and plant species, as well as the stunning sandy beaches. The numerous nature resorts and boutique hotels offer visitors the chance to spend a leisurely vacation near nature. Most of them are located in secluded areas of national parks or forests, beside freshwater streams, along with bungalows on isolated stretches of beach. Few of these locations give luxurious lodging and amenities because they were created with the environment in mind, but all of them offer an exhilarating experience unlike any other. You may easily discover a boutique hotel with regional designs for a fair price that provides you with exceptional service as well as the chance to experience the local cuisine and culture. The island is often endowed with magnificent hotels, often found in breathtaking locations. There are countless resort hotels along the shore, notably in the west and south.
Sri Lanka has the most buzzing and raving nightlife with streets filled with food vendors, musicians, and other forms of entertainment. The variety of nightclubs that come alive as nightfall draws near highlights Sri Lanka’s diversity. If you know where to search, you can find a busy nightlife whether you’re looking for the beach or a busy pub in the center of Colombo. Visitors can enjoy a light lunch or drink while watching the sunset on the beach before heading out to start their evenings with cocktails at bars and dancing the night away at Colombo’s top clubs. You can enjoy a night of entertainment because the bars and clubs stay open until daybreak. If you still want to party, Sri Lanka has amazing 24-hour beach parties that are ideal for raves! Try your luck at the nearby casinos if you’re in the mood for something new!
Sri Lanka has the most unique and tantalizing cuisine that you just cannot miss out on! The food is flavored with traditional spices and herbs making every dish a mouthwatering delicacy! The famous rice and curry spread contains a potent and fragrant lentil curry with rice and veggies on the side, as well as chicken or fish. Locals strongly suggest the spicy black fish curry called the “Ambul thiyal”. Other famous dishes that are a must-try are steaming hoppers, kottu, spicy meat curries, and fresh seafood! Food in Sri Lanka is always one of the top 10 reasons to visit Sri Lanka in 2024 and many years to come!
The largest land mammal, the elephant, and the largest sea mammal, the blue whale, may both be seen in Sri Lanka, which is likely the only site in the world where you can do so! The waters off Kalpitiya are frequently home to five different kinds of whales, including the blue whale and the minke whale. If you’re fortunate, you might also be able to see spinner dolphins. January and March are Kalpitiya’s busiest whale-watching months. This is something that you do not want to miss, do you? This is one of the 10 reasons to visit Sri Lanka in 2024!
Nuwara Eliya, sometimes known as Little England, is a serene town in the central Sri Lanka tea country hills. Though Nuwara Eliya is a rural town, it provides a lot to see and do. It is full of colonial-period structures and bungalows, Tudor-style hotels, and well-kept hedges and gardens, giving the image of a town from a bygone era. Visit Gregory Lake, a sizable body of water, for some water sports and a barbeque cookout, or Horton Grasslands, a lovely grassland with undulating grasslands, waterfalls, and the majestic World’s End cliff. It is recommended that you stay at the Grand Hotel in Nuwara Eliya for luxury comforts, good food, and a unique experience!
Browse our blog to take a sneak peek into our destinations. We have things to do, sights to see, people to meet, food to taste—we hardly ever run out of things to talk about.

## Best Hiking Trails in Sri Lanka

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/blog/best-hiking-trails-in-sri-lanka/

> Discover the best hiking trails in Sri Lanka with scenic views, difficulty levels, and trekking tips for nature lovers. Read more.

![Horton Plains](https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/03/Best-hiking-trails-in-Sri-Lanka-1920x600-1.jpg)
scroll down

Best Hiking Trails in Sri Lanka
July 16, 2025

While Sri Lanka is known for its idyllic, golden beaches, the country also has the most scenic hills and mountains. If you are a passionate hiker, Sri Lanka can promise you a truly unique experience. The country’s name alone conjures up pictures of lush forests, misty mountains, tropical fauna, and azure waters onely Planet named it the “best country in the world” for tourists to visit. In addition to surfing, culture, and hospitality of the people, Sri Lanka is also rich with a variety of wildlife and nature reserves. You can find plenty of emerald mountains and hills that are perfect for hiking with family and friends! Each of these hiking routes is distinctive; some lead you to the top sights to witness a range of biodiversity, while others lead you through tropical rainforests or historic and religious places.
Here are the best hiking trails in Sri Lanka that you need to cross off your bucket list! These beautiful mountains and hills will make you fall in love with Sri Lanka if you enjoy spending your vacation hiking!
Sri Lanka’s most sacred top, also known as Adam’s Peak, Sri Pada, and Samanalakande, is visited by people of all faiths and beliefs. This hike can be a spiritual trip with a climb of 5200 stairs and a 7 km walk from the village of Dalhousie. The peak offers you a peek into Sri Lanka’s stunning vistas of its lush surroundings.
Little Adam’s Peak, regarded as one of the best hiking trails in Sri Lanka, offers breathtaking eagle-eye views of the country’s lush valleys and mist-covered mountain peaks. The southern coast of the island can even be seen from Little Adam’s Peak, according to the locals, on exceptionally clear days.
The largest lowland rainforest in the nation, the Sinharaja Forest Reserve, is a biodiverse wonderland. This is bordered by rivers and a range of animal species that include rare mammals and 147 bird species, 33 of which are exclusive to Sri Lanka. The Sinharaja Forest Reserve can only be entered on foot and is considered one of the best hiking trails in Sri Lanka. You must be geared up with appropriate clothing and equipment to keep you safe in the dense jungle. Many hikers set up camp near the towns of Deniyaya or Kudawa, where you may buy admission tickets and schedule a hike with the tour operators stationed at the reserve entrances.
The sheer drop-off at the edge of the Horton Plains, which overlooks one of Sri Lanka’s most breathtaking vistas, is known as the “World’s End”. Horton Plains National Park is an expanded stretch of lush wilderness in Sri Lanka’s hill countryside. The 9km looped track and hike leads you to the World’s End. You can see where it got its name when you finally reach the stunning 4000-foot-high cliff. The Horton plains are a beautiful home to a range of wildlife. This includes the rare samba deer and leopards! You can also find many exotic bird species while exploring these best hiking trails in Sri Lanka. One of the few parks where people can walk alone is Horton Plains National Park, but as there is no public transportation there, you’ll need to make arrangements for a drop-off and pick-up in Nuwara Eliya or Ohiya. When the weather is fine, the views of the lowlands floating below are spectacular! To witness these moments, you need to arrive early in the morning to avoid the crowds and the clouds. You need around 3 hours to complete the 9.5km journey, which includes a return trip to the lovely Baker’s Falls and a return ride to the Far Inn entrance to the national park.
Diyaluma Falls, which is located in the Badulla area is the second-tallest waterfall in Sri Lanka. It shouldn’t take you more than a couple of hours to reach the summit, although the hike isn’t particularly strenuous. Along the way, there’s a ton to accomplish as well! For instance, swimming in one of the many rock pools is a good way to cool off and relax (but be sure to steer clear of doing so during the rainy season!). T. The magnificent waterfall’s full extent, plunging several hundred feet to the pools below, is visible from the summit.
The Great Western Mountain, which rises to a height of 2,216 meters, is enveloped in forests and heavy plumes of mist. This towers over the Great Western train station and tea estate. The Great Western is the seventh-highest peak and is also one of the best hiking trails in Sri Lanka. Although it is a pleasant trek, it is fairly steep and calls for strength and experience. You can find a Hindu temple with colorful walls that stand out against the surrounding. A set of steps next to the Kovil leads up to the forest and the designated hiking track. From that point on, you’ll be hiking through a thick forest, so a machete will be useful. Depending on how many stops you take and how quickly you trek, the entire hike will take between 5 and 6 hours, and as you ascend, the terrain gets steeper, so a rope can come in handy.

## Best months to visit Sri Lanka

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/blog/best-months-to-visit-sri-lanka/

![Best months to visit Sri Lanka](https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/06/Best-months-to-visit-Sri-Lanka-1920x600-2.jpg)
scroll down

Best months to visit Sri Lanka
June 1, 2023

One of the many good reasons to put Sri Lanka on your bucket list is its tropical climate, stunning beaches, mouthwatering cuisine, and exotic culture. Visit the historic rock of Sigiriya to learn about its history, see Asian elephants in Minneriya National Park, or catch some waves at Arugam Bay or Hikkaduwa in this Jewel of the Indian Ocean! If you want the ideal vacation, Sri Lanka is where you need to be! Similar to the Maldives, its proximity to the equator assures comfortable, consistent temperatures throughout the year. Its yearly dual monsoons also mean that when one half of the country is getting rainfall, the other is ideal for beach afternoons. Also, there are many colourful festivals and holidays on the calendar that offer tourists like you a distinctive cultural highlight every month. Sri Lanka is best described as a place with golden beaches, crashing waves, misty mountains, and exotic marine and wildlife species. With its regal past, exquisite tea, and friendly smiles, a vacation in Sri Lanka is all you need! You may be riding the waves in the morning and enjoying the mountains covered in greenery in the evening thanks to the abundance of sights and landscapes on a small island.
You can find a range of vacation experiences, including pilgrimages to some of the world’s oldest towns as well as sun-kissed beach vacations, and morning wildlife sightings! The best months to visit Sri Lanka depends on where you choose to go because, despite being a relatively small country, there are significant seasonal climate changes. There are two monsoon seasons in Sri Lanka, and they have an impact on various regions during various months. This implies that wherever they go on the island, visitors can anticipate bright and sunny weather at any moment. Because the southwest monsoon occurs between May and July, the north is dry, and because the northeast monsoon occurs between October and January, the south is open for exploration! Here is a guide to the best times to visit Sri Lanka, whether you’re seeking the finest time to visit the beach, going on a wildlife safari or walking to the top of Adam’s Peak, or simply avoiding the tourist crowds. Make sure you plan your trip during the best months to visit Sri Lanka so that you can make the best out of your trip!
The best months to visit Sri Lanka for some waves is during the high season from December to April. The best weather in the south and west of Sri Lanka arrives during the country’s high season, making popular resort areas like Hikkaduwa and Mirissa their most picturesque yet busiest. The East, North, and Ancient Cities regions remain rainy during the Maha monsoon season (October to January).
Although the region’s year-round temperatures range from 27° to 29°C, the Yala Monsoon Season occurs here from April to September. The rainiest at this time are April, May, and June. The majority of beach hotels are located here. October and March are the best months to visit Sri Lanka! November to April is thought to be ideal for surfing on Sri Lanka’s south coast beaches and surfing on the east coast is best from April to October. Popular surfing spots like Pottuvil Point and Arugam Bay come to life at this time. The two shoulder seasons of Sri Lanka, which are wedged between the two significant monsoons, offer the best weather while also being less crowded and more affordable.
The Tamil and Sinhala New Year festivals fall in April, which can interfere with travel around the nation. The weather is typically dry but not as hot as in the height of summer, making this a suitable season for climbing Sigiriya or going on a hike in the Hill Country. This is the best time to hike and explore mother nature! You can also witness many elephants congregate for many weeks in Minneriya National Park in September and occasionally in August. The “The Gathering” event, where infant elephants can also be seen, is regarded as one of Asia’s most incredible wildlife spectacles!
The Yala monsoon arrives in May and pours down on the west and south as well as the Hill Country through August. This marks the beginning of Sri Lanka’s low season. Travel to Jaffna in the north or the laid-back resorts on the east coast, including Arugam Bay, known for its relaxed surf culture, is best during this season. Generally, the lodging costs during this time are lower, and crowds are smaller. Despite the Yala rain, this time of year is filled with exciting celebrations. Vesak Poyas is a two-day festival in May when colorful lights adorn every Buddhist temple and home in Sri Lanka. Thousands of dancers and drummers create a massive parade through Kandy during the energetic Esala Perahera. This is one of the best celebrations in Sri Lanka, and it is something that you wouldn’t want to miss!
January marks the height of the travel season and is one of the best months to visit Sri Lanka. Even though more people are around, the south and west have pleasant weather, making it a generally enticing time to travel. Many important events include the elaborate Duruthu Perahera festival in the nation’s capital, Colombo, which honors the first of Buddha’s trips to Sri Lanka with lights, dances, and parades. You can also visit and join the Galle Literary Festival which is a significant occasion that many people across the nation join.
This is a busy month because of Independence Day, celebrated with festivals, parades, fireworks, and sporting activities all around the country. Independence Day is a major occasion and one of the most important events hosted in Sri Lanka. Be sure to visit Sri Lanka during its celebratory events to ensure your experience in Sri Lanka is worthwhile!

## Family guide to visit Sri Lanka

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/blog/family-guide-to-visit-sri-lanka/

> Plan the perfect family trip to Sri Lanka with kid-friendly beaches, wildlife safaris, culture, and travel tips. Explore more.

![Family guide to visit Sri Lanka](https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/03/Family-guide-to-visit-Sri-Lanka-1920x600-1.jpg)
scroll down

Family guide to visit Sri Lanka
June 1, 2023

Sri Lanka! A beautiful island encrusted in the Indian ocean is best known for its rich cultural diversity and breathtaking natural beauty. Sri Lanka has become one of the most popular and most visited, making it a number one travel destination for you and your family! This beautiful nation is a treasure trove for passionate travellers, surfers and yogis! With its cultural sites, diverse topography, pristine beaches and exotic wildlife, numerous people visit Sri Lanka for its enduring natural beauty. A single trip to Sri Lanka, will guarantee you unforgettable memories and moments that you will treasure for a lifetime!
Planning a visit to Sri Lanka with your family? If you are, it is important that you consider your children’s ages and interests while organising a family trip to Sri Lanka. Keep reading to see a complete family guide to visit Sri Lanka! Many of Sri Lanka’s historic sites and temples might not be appropriate for young children. There are, however, many events and attractions that are ideal for families with children of all ages. Tropical beaches, whale watching, exotic wildlife, misty mountains are some of the places and activities that are a must to do and see if you are visiting Sri Lanka with your family. Additionally, you can also experience tantalising cuisine and an incredibly warm welcome from the people in Sri Lanka! Sri Lanka truly has everything that can make your family trip a worthy one!
Looking for the best places and activities for your family vacation in Sri Lanka? Look no further! Here is a family guide to visit Sri Lanka and its top spots for 2023! Sri Lanka is one of the best family-friendly destinations in Asia, drawing many tourists to its beautiful shores every year. Finding things to do with the kids in this tropical paradise is simple thanks to the diversity of activities available. The breathtaking beaches offer a range of activities for water sports, including surfing lessons and snorkelling with sea turtles. The country’s national parks are home to several wildlife species that may be found further inland in Sri Lanka. With your family, you are able to explore a whole new experience while keeping an eye out for elephants, deer, leopards, and birds. Each of these stunning locations in Sri Lanka has its own allure and would be enthralling to see as part of a road trip itinerary across Sri Lanka that lasts between 5 and 7 days! Here are the best places you cannot miss out on when visiting Sri Lanka as a family!
Some of Sri Lanka’s most stunning beaches may be found in the coastal resort town of Unawatuna. Therefore, it’s not surprising that so many people decide to spend their family vacation in this serene and bustling town. You are also able to find a range of family-friendly lodging options in Sri Lanka to suit all budgets. From affordable hotels to opulent luxury villas, you can find everything you need! In addition, the golden, powdery sands and the crimson sunsets provide the ideal ambiance for relaxing and sunbathing. Unawatuna is also the ideal location for swimming, even if you have young children with you, thanks to its calm, clear waters. However, if you’re looking for the perfect way to spend time with your family, you can enroll your family in surfing lessons. Why not enroll them in a few classes while you’re in town since it’s a great time to bond with your family? Other than the beach, Unawatuna offers many other family-friendly activities.
If you are in Unawatuna, you cannot miss the turtle hatchery! This is an ideal family guide to visit Sri Lanka so you wouldn’t miss out on anything! This is the perfect opportunity for your kids to see endangered turtles and different species of marine wildlife. Here, you can get close to some baby sea turtles. Despite being one of the most significant breeding places in the world, Sri Lanka’s sea turtle population is declining quickly. Therefore, the hatcheries’ conservation efforts are crucial, and kids will like seeing and holding the tiny turtles!
Small waves with a calm surge are ideal for beginning surfers in Unawatuna. The shallow waters and sandy bottom of Unawatuna make it one of the safest areas in Sri Lanka to practice, making it one of the greatest locations to learn the basics. There are many surf schools in the region, making it a wonderful site to take a family surfing class.
The ideal location in Sri Lanka to see some of the most breathtaking wildlife is Mirissa. Many whale species, notably the majestic Blue whale, cross this region of Sri Lanka’s south coast when migrating. The best time for these magnificent creatures, along with dolphins, is between October and March. These waters are frequently home to sperm whales, and killer whales, all of which can be observed during a whale-watching tour from Mirissa. This is a must-do if you’re traveling with family as it truly is an incredible experience. This is a must to do in your family guide to visit Sri Lanka!
Want the perfect family guide to visit Sri Lanka? One of the most well-known safari destinations in Sri Lanka is the Yala National Park, which is located in Yala. If you wish to see as much amazing wildlife as you could with your family, this is the perfect opportunity! You can see some of the most spectacular animals quite close, from wild elephants, and sambar deer to the elusive leopard. There is much more to see and do in Yala outside of the National Park. It’s a great option for adventurous families with a ton of places to explore! You can also set up a camp forest for a few nights in Yala for a memorable family adventure. Although it is not permitted to camp inside the park, there are several locations where you can do so while still enjoying the forest’s tropical beauty. While visiting Yala, consider renting a tent for your family to have a truly memorable outdoor experience!
Nuwara Eliya is the highest town in Sri Lanka and is one of the most beautiful and popular destination spots both for tourists and locals. The Nuwara Eliya Golf Club, racetrack, Victoria Park, and trout fishing in adjacent lakes and rivers keep the town’s reputation as “Little England” alive. Due to its chilly weather, strawberry farms, and vintage hotels, this is an ideal place for you to visit with family and kids. There are many kid-friendly activities in Nuwara Eliya to entertain the most energetic kids! Everyone has something to do in Nuwara Eliya, whether it is exploring the town’s many beautifully kept gardens, learning about the colonial history of the region, or hiking via mountain trails! You can enjoy horse rides, boat rides, safaris, and strawberry picking during your stay.
The Ambewela Farm, also known as “Little New Zealand,” is situated in a gorgeous setting, surrounded by luxuriant vegetation and mist-covered hilltops. Along with other farm animals, you can see a range of cows and cattle grazing here! If you’re lucky, you might witness the cows being milked. Dairy items straight from the farm are also available. The farm is about 20 kilometers from Nuwara Eliya; it can be reached there in 45 minutes by car from the city. This is a great educational and fun-filled activity that you and your family can enjoy during your stay in Sri Lanka!
Browse our blog to take a sneak peek into our destinations. We have things to do, sights to see, people to meet, food to taste—we hardly ever run out of things to talk about.
