## Is it safe to travel to Sri Lanka?

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/blog/is-it-safe-to-travel-to-sri-lanka/

> Find out how safe Sri Lanka is for travelers with updated tips on security, weather, and local travel advice. Read more.

![Sigiriya Sri Lanka](https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/07/Sigiriya-1920x600-1.jpg)
scroll down

Is it safe to travel to Sri Lanka?
July 16, 2025

If you are wondering whether it would be safe to travel to Sri Lanka in 2025, the answer is yes. The safety of tourists in Sri Lanka has improved significantly over the past few years. The government has taken steps to ensure that visitors have a safe and secure experience during their stay in Sri Lanka. Additionally, the country has implemented strict security measures at airports, hotels, and other tourist attractions. With these precautions, travellers can rest assured that their trip will be enjoyable and safe. You must know that Sri Lanka is usually regarded as a safe country to travel to for single women, families, and individuals in general. Yet, it is always advisable to exercise a few safety measures when travelling. Always be sure to review the most recent headlines and travel warnings because situations can suddenly change. Also, always be mindful of your surroundings and abide by local safety regulations to ensure your vacation is a pleasant experience!
Traveling to Sri Lanka now will allow you to gradually explore every part of the island and immerse yourself in the distinctive experiences it has to offer, as there are often fewer tourists here at this time of year. Fuel is available, hotels and attractions are open, and tourists may enjoy scenic road excursions around the nation to discover its wonders. Many fresh experiences are just waiting to be explored; they combine history, culture, and wildlife against a backdrop of breathtaking scenery.
Here is all that you need to know about how safe Sri Lanka can be. The island is worth visiting for three main reasons. Specifically, the variety of activities, the year-round pleasant weather, and the affordability make it incredibly safe to spend your stay. In addition, the abundance of wildlife and natural resources of Sri Lanka, which is unmatched by other nations in the region, is one of the key attractions. For a distinctive and enthralling vacation experience on this beautiful island, soak some sun and sand in the beautiful beaches embedded in the south coast of Sri Lanka. You may also enjoy a hike in the misty cool mountains to experience Sri Lanka’s enthralling beauty and divine glory at its finest.
It can be overwhelming to plan a trip, but, you can ensure your trip is safe and fun with the correct planning and information. You must look into Sri Lanka’s travel regulations and limitations before planning your trip. It’s crucial to educate yourself about the country’s travel policies before making any travel arrangements. Here you can see that certain aspects make it safe to travel to Sri Lanka. This can help you to organize a secure and enjoyable trip to Sri Lanka, including everything from learning about travel restrictions to choosing the best hotels and preparing for cultural differences.
Political stability returned to Sri Lanka in July 2022, making it completely safe for tourism. Overall, it is deemed safe for people around the world to travel to Sri Lanka. A few months ago, According to the Sri Lanka Tourism Development Authority, it is stated that in February 2023, 107,639 international visitors arrived in Sri Lanka even after the political turmoil that took place in 2022. This growth was driven by the peak travel season as well as other factors including improving socioeconomic conditions and better connectivity to the nation. The majority of tourists visited Europe (67.7%), followed by Asia and the Pacific Region (24.6%), the USA (5.4%), the Middle East (1.7%), and Africa (1.7%). (0.3 percent). Tourist arrivals grew by 5% every month. Throughout the first two months of the year, there were 210,184 more visitors than there were in the same period last year, an increase of 17.5%.
Sri Lanka offers a warm and sunny climate and pleasant weather all year round. Every day is summer in Sri Lanka! The tropical climate of Sri Lanka is marked by both dry and wet seasons. The greatest season to visit the east coast is from April/May to September, while the best time to visit the west and south coasts, as well as the central area, which includes Kandy, and Colombo, is from December to March. During these months, Sri Lanka is typically bathed in sunshine and is comparatively dry throughout, making it an excellent time to tour the entire nation. Even though it will be slightly cooler in the Hill Country, it will be a pleasant and warm day. The South and west coasts provide dry, warm, and sunny beaches, making it safe to travel to Sri Lanka.
In general, it is safe to travel to Sri Lanka in any situation.. However, it is best to avoid walking alone in dark, poorly lit locations if you wish to avoid unwanted attention. In addition, it is recommended to take all necessary safety precautions wherever you go.Sri Lanka is an ideal destination to travel alone or with others due to its hospitable people and humble culture.
Browse our blog to take a sneak peek into our destinations. We have things to do, sights to see, people to meet, food to taste—we hardly ever run out of things to talk about.

## Sailing Tour on the South Coast of Sri Lanka

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/blog/sailing-tour-on-the-south-coast-of-sri-lanka/

> Experience a scenic sailing tour along Sri Lanka’s South Coast with beaches, marine life, and ocean adventures. Explore more.

![Sailing Tour on the South Coast of Sri Lanka](https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/07/Sailing-Tour-on-the-South-1920x600-1.jpg)
scroll down

Sailing Tour on the South Coast of Sri Lanka
July 16, 2025

Are you dreaming of a seacation in Sri Lanka? You probably know that Sri Lanka is famous for its pristine beaches, luxury hotels, joyous festivities, and its rich culture. But have you ever thought about taking a sailing tour on the South coast of Sri Lanka? Because you most certainly need to! Sri Lanka has a lot to offer as an island with azure waters, calm waves, and breathtaking scenery. This is especially true when it comes to the country’s amazing south coast – it is what Sri Lanka is most famous for! You can enjoy the most beautiful beaches, see incredible marine life, and stop at a number of popular tourist destinations enroute!
Sailing along the South coast of Sri Lanka promises a journey where you can unwind and take in the island’s tropical beauty while experiencing breathtaking sceneries and unforgettable moments. You can opt for an overnight sailing tour or even for a 7-day vacation right on the sea.
To ensure a comfortable and enjoyable sailing experience, consider packing these essentials.Sunscreen– Bring more than you anticipate needing. You probably will be in the sun the whole day, and the last things you want are blisters and a heat stroke while you are on your sailing tour!
- Swimwear – bring some of your fancy swimsuits and maybe some extra too. You never know how many beaches you can have a dip in. It is also important to pack some baggy and light clothing to beat the heat of the sun. Some flip-flops, sunglasses and a hat are also important.Grab a shawl or a jumper or two to wear through the cold and chilly nights. Seasickness medication –Over the counter medication is recommended for individuals prone to motion sickness or for longer sailing tours.
- Pack some modest clothing – To respect the people, culture, and sanctity of sacred temples and monuments, you would be required to cover your shoulders and body below the knees. This is especially important if you are taking a quick tour through the local temples enroute.
Typically, a 7-day Sailing Tour offers the ideal balance of relaxation and exploration as you explore the tropical beauty that lies on the south coast of Sri Lanka. However, you have the option of sailing on the South coast of Sri Lanka within a day too! You’ll be sailing through Sri Lanka’s southern coast by stopping at iconic places like the fishing village of Kudawella, secluded beaches, Dondra head lighthouse, local temples, and the Galle Fort. You are also able to experience the beauty of crimson sunrises and sunsets! However, visiting the beaches is definitely the highlight of your sailing tour! You can cruise through Weligama Bay, Jungle Beach at Unawatuna, Tallala Beach, Kudawella, and Mirissa Beach, to mention a few. Your tour may entail the best dolphin and whale-watching locations along the coast, depending on the itinerary you select and the length of your stay.
You can see the world’s largest mammal, the blue whale, commonly spotted in Sri Lanka’s beaches between November and April once a year. You will be only able to see dolphins and whales cruising their way through on the beaches of Sri Lanka! This is a must to do when you plan your trip to the South coast of Sri Lanka.
You can also enjoy swimming, surfing, snorkelling, and enjoying the scenery when your feet start to get tired. You will enjoy an unforgettable experience in Sri Lanka’s stunning waters thanks to the abundance of amazing marine life there, including whales and dolphins. You can also catch some waves while surfing in the tropical bay of Weligama and wake up to the sight of the sunrise which provides you with an unforgettably relaxing seaside getaway!
Here are some things you can do and enjoy during your 7-day excursion on your Sailing tour;
This is the first day of your exciting sailing adventure! You can unwind and mingle with your fellow travellers! You will be served breakfast on board that is fine with freshly cut tropical fruits, bread, cheese, meats, and some hot Ceylon tea. This is definitely the perfect start to your amazing day.
After a hearty breakfast, you can set sail for Weligama. Travel to Kudawella while taking in breathtaking sights and stopping at some secluded beaches to surf, swim, Paddleboard, or kayak. Keep a close lookout for whales and dolphins throughout the sailing journey.
Keep sailing up the South coast to Kalametiya to wander around the lagoon. You can have a quick visit to the humble Kalametiya village and sanctuary in the late afternoon. You can also catch some waves, relax or work on your tab while soaking in some sun!
Surf or swim at the little surf beach of Hiriketiya before continuing to Talalla Beach on Sri Lanka’s southern coast. Keep looking out for exotic marine life along the Sailing tour on your way from Kalametiya to Talalla.
On your way to Rumassala, keep an eye out for whales and dolphins. Then, spend the afternoon chilling on Jungle Beach or ascending to the Japanese Peace Pagoda for magnificent views of Galle Bay.
Set sail by morning with a hearty breakfast and lunch that will be served on board as it docks in Galle at noon. You can have a stroll around the Galle Fort in the late afternoon. Dinner will be usually provided on board with a view of the city lights of Galle.
It is time for your adventurous sailing tour on the South coast of Sri Lanka to end! Go back to Mirissa Harbour and end your journey. You can arrange for transport or a private vehicle to pick you up to either go back to the airport or your hotel.
Browse our blog to take a sneak peek into our destinations. We have things to do, sights to see, people to meet, food to taste—we hardly ever run out of things to talk about.

## Sri Lanka’s tourism prediction in 2024

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/blog/sri-lankas-tourism-prediction-in-2024/

> Explore Sri Lanka’s tourism outlook for 2024, visitor trends, recovery insights, and what travelers can expect. Read more.

![Sigiriya rock](https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/06/Sri-Lankas-tourism-prediction-in-2024-1920x600-1.jpg)
scroll down

Sri Lanka’s tourism prediction in 2024
June 1, 2023

Sri Lanka is a destination that is growing in popularity, but forecasting the number of tourists the nation will welcome in 2024 requires a thorough study of the current status of tourism, important factors that will affect it, and potential changes in tourist behavior. Here, you will be able to look at how to forecast Sri Lanka’s tourism prediction in 2024. This can be done by assessing the current situation of the industry, understanding what made the tourism industry deteriorate from 2020, and estimating predicted growth or decline in tourists in the future. Sri Lanka has seen a significant increase in the number of tourist arrivals for January 2023, compared to the numbers seen in January 2022. In an attempt to revitalize the economy, the new government and officials are trying their best to boost tourist arrivals to Sri Lanka. This is achieved by expanding the hotel sectors, constructing new properties, and providing new custom amenities, offers, and services. There are many boutique hotels on the West and south coast emerging to cater to surfers and honeymooners. Sri Lanka’s tourism sector is crucial to the nation’s economy and has a huge potential to increase wealth and advance inclusive sustainable development. Because of this and despite the current crisis, the airlines are running on schedule, and the flight of passengers is unaffected by the refueling of aircraft at international gateways.
Assessing the Current Tourism Situation in Sri Lanka
The Covid-19 pandemic outbreak in 2020 had a significant negative impact on Sri Lanka’s tourism industry, with visitor arrivals falling by more than 70% compared to 2019. Between March and May 2020, the country’s borders were closed, and only a small amount of domestic movement was permitted. As a result, there was a sharp decline in hotel occupancy, aircraft reservations, and tour packages. Around 500 thousand foreign tourists arrived in Sri Lanka in 2020. According to data published by the World Bank for International Visitors Arrivals (IVA), there was a striking decrease from the 2 million visitors recorded for the same year in 2019. This includes tourists who came solely for pleasure as well as business people and visa applicants. These tourists might have been stranded as a result of travel restrictions imposed by their governments or cancellation of visa requests as a result of Covid-19-related problems like reduced airline capacity or regional lockdowns within their own countries. As was to be expected, the majority of these visitors were Europeans, who accounted for nearly half of all IVA with Asia coming in second place with 41%. North Americans and people from other regions of Africa combined made up the majority of the remaining 9% of the population.
In July 2022, Sri Lanka saw a return to political stability, making travel there entirely risk-free. In general, it is considered safe for tourists from all around the world to visit Sri Lanka now. Sri Lankan civilians attempted to speak out against the corrupt politicians and administration a few months ago. A new president, prime minister, and significant cabinet members are currently attending to the nation’s primary problems and safety measures. Despite the widespread unrest, neither Sri Lankan tourists nor the tourism industry was harmed in any way. Even after the political unrest that occurred in 2022, the Sri Lanka Tourist Development Authority reports that 107,639 foreign visitors visited Sri Lanka in February of 2023. Peak travel season, as well as other variables including increasing socioeconomic conditions and more accessibility to the country, were the primary factors of this development. There were 210,184 more visitors during the first two months of the year than there were during the same time last year. With a new government, Sri Lanka’s tourism prediction in 2024, looks promising and feasible.
Sri Lanka’s tourist sector resurrected immediately after the widespread protest and political chaos that occurred last year. There is hope with tourism numbers increasing, but they are still well below official goals currently. The tourist industry in Sri Lanka contributes significantly to the island country’s economy, accounting for 12% of its GDP. The Sri Lankan economy gains thousands of new direct and indirect jobs as a result of the tourism industry. Over 106,500 tourists entered Sri Lanka in March 2022, a significant rise from the 4,581 tourists that arrived in the month in March 2021. However, because of the island’s economic turmoil, tourist arrivals fell to 30,207 by May 2022. One aspect of Sri Lanka’s declining foreign exchange reserves was the decline in tourist visitors brought on by the COVID-19 pandemic. So, according to economic specialists, if there is a rise in tourist arrivals, it could help the country out of its current economic difficulty.
Sri Lanka’s tourism prediction in 2024 is 3 million and presently aims to draw an estimated 1.5 million visitors by the end of 2023. Even after the political unrest that occurred in 2022, the Sri Lanka Tourist Development Authority reports that 107,639 foreign visitors visited Sri Lanka in February of 2023. Peak travel season, as well as other variables including increasing socioeconomic conditions and more accessibility to the country, were the main drivers of this development. There were 210,184 more visitors during the first two months of this year than there were during the same time last year, which is a rise of 17.5%. The Sri Lankan tourism sector is crucial to the nation’s economy and has a huge potential to increase wealth and enhance inclusive, sustainable development. Because of this and despite the current crisis, the government, officials, and the tourism industry of Sri Lanka are working hard to create a welcoming atmosphere for tourists. Currently, Sri Lanka’s coming up with an extensive national plan to develop the service industry, particularly tourism, and launch an industrialization program. Because they think that doing so will boost promotional success, convey a positive message, and reinstate Sri Lanka as a leading tourist destination, which is hope! The above information on how Sri Lanka’s Tourism prediction in 2024 looks hopeful will surely be convincing enough for you to plan your visit to Sri Lanka! You can explore the much-spoken-about holiday destination without any hesitation anymore! Whether you are looking for relaxation, picturesque scenery, thrilling adventure, vibrant culture, and good food, Sri Lanka has it all!
Browse our blog to take a sneak peek into our destinations. We have things to do, sights to see, people to meet, food to taste—we hardly ever run out of things to talk about.

## Cancellation & Payment Policy

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/cancellation-payment-policy/

> Understand Mosvold Hotels’ cancellation, payment terms, refund rules, seasonal policies, and no-show charges before booking. Learn more.

At Mosvold Boutique Hotels, we strive to offer flexible booking terms while ensuring fairness to all guests. The following cancellation and payment policies apply to direct online bookings. Please note: promotional rates and special offers may carry stricter conditions that override the terms below.
Peak Season Bookings
Applicable Dates: 15th December to 15th January
Payment Terms:
A deposit of 100% of the total stay is required at the time of booking.
Cancellation Terms:
Cancellations within 07 days of the arrival date – 100% of the value of the booking
Cancellations within 14 days of the arrival date – 50% of the value of the booking
Cancellations within 21 days of the arrival date – 25% of the value of the booking
Cancellations during Peak (15th Dec – 15th Jan) – 100% of the value of the booking ( within 21 days of the arrival date )
Regular Season Bookings
Applicable Dates: 16th January to 14th December
Payment Terms:
A deposit of 100% of the total stay is required at the time of booking.
Cancellation Terms:
Cancellations within 07 days of the arrival date – 100% of the value of the booking
Cancellations within 14 days of the arrival date – 50% of the value of the booking
Cancellations within 21 days of the arrival date – 25% of the value of the booking
No shows:
All no-shows will be charged 100% of the full stay
Checkin Checkouts
Please note that the check-in time & check out time
Check-in: 2:00 PM / 1400 h
Check-out: 11:00 AM / 1100 h

## Privacy Policy

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/privacy-policy/

> Learn how Mosvold Hotels collects, uses, and protects your personal data, cookies, and privacy rights when using our website. Read more.

![Privacy Policy](https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/07/Sundara-by-Mosvold-1920x600-1.jpg)
scroll down

At Mosvold Boutique Hotels, we are committed to protecting your personal information and ensuring transparency in how we collect, use, and safeguard your data when you visit our website or engage with our services/products.
We may collect personal details from you when you interact with our website, make a booking, or register for services. This information may include:
– Your name
– Email address
– Contact number
– Payment details
– Other information you voluntarily provide
We gather information when you:
– Make a reservation
– Subscribe to our newsletter
– Complete a form on our website
– Participate in a promotion or survey
– Contact our team or provide feedback
Your information may be used in the following ways:
– To enhance your guest experience and tailor our services to your preferences
– To improve our website and customer support
– To process bookings and transactions efficiently
– To communicate relevant offers, updates, or service-related messages
– To respond to your inquiries or requests for assistance
We prioritize the protection of your personal information. Our website is regularly monitored for security vulnerabilities. Sensitive information is encrypted via Secure Socket Layer (SSL) technology and is only accessible by authorized personnel under strict confidentiality obligations.
All transactions are processed through secure payment gateways; no payment information is stored on our servers.
We do not sell, trade, or share your personally identifiable information with third parties.
Our website does not feature third-party products or services.
We are committed to protecting the privacy of children. We do not knowingly collect personal information from individuals under the age of 13. Our website and services are not directed toward children.
You may adjust how Google displays ads to you by visiting the Google Ad Settings page or opt out using the Network Advertising Initiative Opt-Out page or the Google Analytics Opt-Out browser add-on.
You will be notified of any changes to this privacy policy on this page.
To update or request removal of your personal information, please email us directly.
Mosvold Hotels respects Do Not Track (DNT) signals. We do not plant tracking cookies or serve ads when DNT settings are enabled in your browser.
We use third-party tools such as the Facebook Pixel to better understand visitor interactions and deliver relevant advertising. These tools may track your behavior across our website and others. Data collected is subject to the privacy policies of the respective third parties.
If you have any questions or concerns regarding this privacy policy, please contact us:
Phone: +94 77 335 8800
Email: [email protected]

## Terms & Conditions

*URL:* https://www.mosvoldhotels.com/terms-conditions/

> Review Mosvold Hotels’ terms and conditions covering bookings, guest responsibilities, policies, and legal guidelines. Learn more.

Why Book Directly With Us
Enjoy an exceptional booking experience when you reserve directly through the official Mosvold Boutique Hotels website. From the moment you book, our dedicated reservations team ensures seamless service and personalized attention to every request or change. Direct bookings come with exclusive benefits, the most competitive rates, and priority flexibility—making your stay with us truly unforgettable.
Best Rate Guarantee
We proudly offer a Best Rate Guarantee on all direct bookings made through our website. This means:
1.You’ll receive the lowest publicly available rate for your room, including taxes and fees.
2.You benefit from the most flexible cancellation and amendment policies.
3.Our reservations team is available to help you plan and customize your stay.
Check-In & Check-Out Times
Check-in: 2:00 PM / 1400 h
Check-out: 11:00 AM /1100 h
We kindly ask that you inform us in advance of any special arrival or departure requirements. We’ll do our best to accommodate early check-ins or late check-outs, but it’s subject to availability.
Exclusive Offers & Packages
Unlock exclusive benefits and tailored experiences available only through our official website. These may include curated packages, complimentary upgrades, or even a chance to win a free stay-available only to direct bookers.
No Hidden Charges
All prices displayed on our official website are transparent and include applicable taxes and fees. When you book with Mosvold Boutique Hotels, you won’t encounter any hidden charges.
Privacy & Data Protection
Your personal information is treated with the utmost confidentiality. We do not sell or share your data with third parties. For more details, refer to our Privacy Policy.
Flexibility & Support
Direct bookings are granted priority handling when it comes to modifications, special requests, and customer support. We understand that travel plans can change-and we’re here to accommodate you wherever possible.
Please note that Mosvold Boutique Hotels requires all local guests to present a valid NIC or Sri Lankan Passport at the time of check-in for all resident bookings.
